Preserve that intellectual continuity through a short note if the main sentence is trimmed. Check that content-triggering, semantic usefulness, and causal value remain separate claims. |

#### Cultural Scope of the Training Corpus · [L3891](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:3891>)

**Goal:** Expose cultural dependence in the Core's ordinary-language values and specify plural validation.
**Verdict: Met.** The subsection clearly identifies how cultural assumptions enter and how reflective traces could either expose or legitimize them. The validation proposal is proportionate to a research program. Its remaining gap is how unresolved disagreements among traditions will be reported and used, rather than collapsed into a single apparently universal score.

**Pre-edit risk — High.** Disaggregating cultural judgments can preserve disagreement, but converting “broadly human” concerns into whatever raters agree on would replace a substantive ethical aspiration with procedure. Preserve both the possibility of cultural triangulation and the risk of fluent cultural rationalization. Add disagreement reporting without declaring consensus sufficient or all perspectives equivalent. Ask the author how unresolved normative conflict should affect selection; check that plural validation does not silently redefine care, wisdom, or human concern.

A cross-reference to the later semantic trusted-base discussion could prevent this introductory point from being restated there at equal length. | **Low.** Cross-referencing semantic inheritance could make cultural breadth look like a minor example of word ambiguity rather than a substantive issue about whose values shape the Student. Retain the cultural mechanism locally. Use the reference only for the broader dependency; check that people, traditions, and power remain visible rather than becoming corpus statistics alone. |
| [U498 · L3895](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:3895>) · “Cultural triangulation…” | Weigh reflective visibility against fluent rationalization and require plural validation. | Met | This is balanced without becoming indecisive: the same mechanism can reveal or launder assumptions, so culturally diverse sources and evaluators are necessary to tell which. The proposed distinction between broadly human concerns and parochial defaults remains contested. Preserve disagreements and report disaggregated judgments instead of presupposing one consensual boundary. | **High.** Replacing a broadly human/parochial distinction with disaggregated ratings could erase the aspiration to identify real shared human concerns; retaining one score could erase disagreement. Preserve both tensions. Add plural judgments as evidence rather than a substitute ethical theory, and ask the author how conflicts should be represented or adjudicated before changing the validation criterion. |

#### Graph-Specific Risks · [L3897](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:3897>)

**Goal:** Show how persistent retrieval can entrench mistakes or disrupt the Student's evaluative policy.
**Verdict: Partly.** The second failure is mechanistically specific and tied to a test. The first treats graph connectivity as if it automatically confers epistemic authority and invokes an unspecified decay mitigation. State the retrieval/ranking/supersession rule that creates inertia, and distinguish annotation-graph risks from optional live-deployment risks.

**Pre-edit risk — Medium.** Specifying retrieval and supersession can turn a conceptual warning into an unwarrantedly narrow bug tied to one implementation. Preserve the possibility that connected commitments resist revision and that live retrieval conflicts with learned judgment. State a plausible mechanism conditionally and distinguish graph-at-annotation from graph-at-deployment. Check that a successful bounded retrieval test does not imply the broader epistemic-inertia concern is eliminated, and leave mitigation design open unless the author selects it.

The text-buffer comparison and “non-zero risk” add little precision. #### Teacher Alignment as Prerequisite · [L3907](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:3907>)

**Goal:** Expose dependence on Teacher quality and the limits of human-baseline validation.
**Verdict: Partly.** The argument successfully rejects prompt compliance as an alignment guarantee and identifies shared evaluator blind spots. “Teacher Alignment as Prerequisite” is broader than what the paragraphs establish: safe, useful annotations do not require globally establishing Teacher alignment, and expert depth does not establish it either. Rename or narrow the prerequisite to the actual gate being tested.

**Pre-edit risk — High.** Renaming Teacher Alignment as Prerequisite around annotation quality could erase the deeper bootstrap worry: a misaligned Teacher may curate convincingly benevolent formative traces. Preserve that strategic and normative concern separately from insufficient expertise and shared cultural blind spots. If narrowing the title, retain an explicit alignment-dependence paragraph. Check that expert depth, harm rubrics, and prompt compliance are each described as partial evidence and never substituted for the unresolved Teacher-alignment premise.

The final Cognitive Buffer Zone sentence introduces a separate causal issue abruptly. Either explain that connection or let this paragraph end with the semantic-quality requirement it has already established. | **Medium.** Removing the Cognitive Buffer Zone sentence could sever the connection between substantive Teacher failure and a central proposed protective mechanism. Preserve the example of formally compliant but shallow care and the claim that framing alone is not a causal buffer. Explain which dimensions Test 1 assesses and add targeted harm review where depth ratings are insufficient. | **High.** Expanding Test 1 into harm review could imply that enough rubrics establish Teacher alignment, while narrowing “detect this” could soften the shared-blind-spot problem. Preserve that human evaluators may miss the same harms as the Teacher. Add distinct tested dimensions and explicit residual limits; check that neither expertise nor cultural plurality is presented as a complete alignment certificate. |

### Reader-Core Specification Uncertainty · [L3924](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:3924>)

**Goal:** Expose the Core as a specific, revisable value specification rather than a neutral phrasing of established requirements.
**Verdict: Met.** This subsection contains several of the paper's most productive limitations: intervention dimensions, technical non-interference, negation/register, semantic inheritance, and omitted truthfulness. Each can change treatment design. It is somewhat repetitive about the untested status of wording; the unresolved scope of future people should be elevated to an explicit specification decision before annotation criteria are frozen.

**Pre-edit risk — High.** Compressing repeated uncertainty or resolving scope can erase the fact that the Core is a value specification whose words, presence, loss assignment, and omissions are consequential choices. Preserve the distinct empirical, semantic, cultural, and normative uncertainties. Future-human scope and any proposed extension require an explicit author decision; measurement alone cannot choose them. Prefer a specification-decision register that leaves unresolved entries open. Check that each wording experiment retains the intended intuition and that no edit silently turns an illustrative variant into the evaluated treatment.

#### Universal Presence, Selective Salience, and Technical Interference · [L3930](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:3930>)

**Goal:** Separate constant Core exposure from context-sensitive evaluation and define when the treatment causes technical harm.
**Verdict: Met.** The decomposition is precise, and the low-relevance non-interference condition prevents unfalsifiable demands that every clause visibly change every trace. The LLaDA controls and explicit failure criterion close the argument well. Clarify the rule for Student loss on Core tokens, one of the opening dimensions that remains less resolved than the others.

**Pre-edit risk — High.** Clarifying loss assignment and condensing interference analysis can silently change Total Saturation or redefine a healthy Core as one that produces no visible effects. Preserve universal context availability and full recital, context-sensitive salience, the risk of hidden human stakes, and technical non-interference on genuinely low-relevance material. Ask the author to choose unresolved loss semantics. Check that independent relevance judgments prevent both forced moralization and retrospective exemption of failed Core activation; the positive safety rationale must survive alongside technical-harm criteria.

#### Testing the Borrowed-Mortality Origin Claim · [L3983](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:3983>)

**Goal:** Isolate the proposed textual origin of self-preservation from the broader intervention and competing causes.
**Verdict: Met.** The section is usefully specific: it declines to infer origin from observed resistance and proposes a small test that can precede the entire pipeline. Its discrimination would improve if it separated exposure to death anxiety from general threat, loss, and self-reference, but the core rhetorical goal is fulfilled without requiring that experiment now.

**Pre-edit risk — Medium.** Refining the small exposure ablation could strip Borrowed Mortality of its broader intuition that human mortality enters formative language, reducing it to a narrow word-count intervention. Preserve the textual-origin hypothesis, competing nontextual persistence routes, and the distinction between motivation and evidence. Add controls as ways to discriminate, not definitions of the whole phenomenon. Check that a negative narrow ablation is not described as disproving every mortality-inheritance mechanism, nor a positive one as validating the full Core.

The complexity-matched removal is a useful control; specify how death anxiety is separated from correlated threat or goal-loss content when turning this into a preregistration. | **Medium.** Separating death anxiety from threat, loss, and self-reference may improve attribution but can dismantle the correlated textual pattern the hypothesis actually concerns. Preserve Borrowed Mortality as a family of inherited meanings, not merely keywords. Ask the author which exposure construct is intended and compare narrow and broader controls; check that conclusions stay within the manipulation’s scope. |

#### Anthropomorphic Language as a Design Choice · [L3999](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:3999>)

**Goal:** Explain why human experiential language was chosen while admitting its semantic and ontological risks.
**Verdict: Met.** The rationale, liabilities, and machine-ontology comparator form a complete argument. The section treats the wording as a design choice rather than evidence of experience, which is essential. Its bridge variant should be cross-linked with the later split-consciousness discussion so the same mitigation is not developed twice.

**Pre-edit risk — High.** Standardizing a machine-ontology bridge or merging it with later dissonance discussion could quietly endorse one ontology and weaken the first-person identity mechanism. Preserve why ordinary human words may recruit rich representations, why technical translations are also contestable, and why ambiguity may both help and harm. Treat the bridge as a prospective comparator requiring author-approved wording. Check that no rewrite asserts either machine experience or its impossibility, and retain the possible cost to the intended Reader stance.

It makes a technical rewrite a competing specification rather than an obviously safer translation. This earns the variant experiment by identifying what the current wording is meant to preserve and what it might distort. | **No edit.** Retain breadth versus fidelity, the contestability of technical translation, and the untested wording choice; no substantive edit is required. |

Preserve the bridge as an unproven alternative with a potential cost. Obtain the author’s wording decision before formalizing a condition; check that it neither denies nor asserts machine experience by implication. |

#### The Negation Problem · [L4013](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:4013>)

**Goal:** Assess whether repeatedly negating fear recruits unwanted associations and define a discriminating wording comparison.
**Verdict: Met.** The section articulates both a priming concern and an inoculation alternative, then gives outcomes that would change the clause. Its argument is empirically open rather than rhetorical. It could distinguish token presence, feature activation, and behavioral influence more explicitly to prevent a representational measure from being mistaken for harm.

**Pre-edit risk — Medium.** Separating token presence, features, register, and behavior could improve precision but lose the powerful double-edged intuition that guaranteed recurrence also guarantees presentation of fear. Preserve both priming and inoculation readings, positive-wording liabilities, and the empirical openness of the comparison. Add distinctions rather than replacing the metaphor with a measurement-only account. Check that reduced feature activation is not automatically equated with safety and that null activation results do not erase the semantic-register concern.

Separate association, speaker register, and behavior, and present corpus-neighborhood inspection as a low-cost precursor to causal testing. | **Medium.** Splitting association, register, and behavior may fragment their combined intuition: recurring first-person fearlessness can carry the very framing it seeks to dissolve. Preserve the window-coverage symmetry and distinctive speaker-register concern. #### The Semantic Trusted Base · [L4044](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:4044>)

**Goal:** Reveal the large inherited semantic dependency beneath a short textual kernel and identify scope-sensitive audits.
**Verdict: Met.** This is a strong conceptual contribution because it explains why minimal wording does not produce a minimal trusted semantic base. The distributive-care example and annotation audit make the concern concrete. The final future-person scope issue is material enough to require a declared intended interpretation and comparator, rather than being left as an incidental closing caveat.

**Pre-edit risk — High.** A shorter semantic-dependency account or an operational scope audit could erase how radically a seven-sentence specification depends on inherited human meaning. Preserve corpus bias, semantic change over time, adversarial reinterpretation, distributive-versus-aggregate care, and unresolved care for future people as distinct issues. Future-human scope is an authorial ethical decision, not a correction the reviewer can supply. Add a scope question register and retain worked examples as partial enacted specification. Check that audit categories do not become a substitute dictionary or imply that scope uncertainty is solved by counting phrases.

#### Core Length and Omitted Values · [L4081](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:4081>)

**Goal:** Frame Core length and omissions as testable design choices while preserving the independence of H1 and H2.
**Verdict: Met.** The section avoids an impossible global-optimality burden and provides a concrete honesty extension with a parity prediction. The first two paragraphs partly repeat the same motivated-but-untested claim. Compress them and retain the distinction between failure of the seven sentences, failure of any recurrent Core, and failure of reader augmentation.

**Pre-edit risk — High.** Combining the motivated-but-untested paragraphs can blur distinct claims: recurrence cost, value completeness, global optimality, and independence of H1/H2 from Core failure. Adding an honesty trade-off rule can also select ethical priorities without authorization. Preserve all claim boundaries and the exact prospective eighth clause. Prefer trimming repeated status phrases while retaining distinct rationales. Before defining “offsetting costs,” ask the author which harms cannot be traded away; verify that truthfulness remains both a philosophical derivation question and an empirical comparator.

### The Risks of Engineering a Self · [L4116](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:4116>)

**Goal:** Analyze risks created by the proposed self-model without making consciousness a prerequisite for harm.
**Verdict: Partly.** The opening establishes a sound behavioral scope, and later paragraphs identify concrete tensions around fear, care, ontology, and self-attachment. But empathy-without-vulnerability, the Vulnerability Gap, split consciousness, and alien wisdom repeatedly revisit overlapping authenticity concerns. Organize this around distinct measurable failures and separate the residual subjective question; several evocative names currently outnumber distinct tests.

**Pre-edit risk — High.** My earlier consolidation advice is especially risky: subjective care, vulnerability-grounded motivation, ontological tension, fear suppression, threat denial, bounded attachment, and alien relevance are not versions of one behavioral failure. Preserve each intuition and its distinct epistemic status, including concerns no present test can resolve. Prefer an additive map with cross-references and only trim literal repetition. Before any merge, require a paragraph-level preservation inventory; verify that authentic concern remains distinct from robust policy, ontology from deception, and ethical uncertainty from measurable performance.

|---|---|---|---| --- |

That qualification prevents the coming discussion from depending on a consciousness claim. Keep this scope visible in the subsection titles as well; “Split Consciousness” later implies a stronger phenomenon than the opening commits to. | **Medium.** Renaming later headings to match the behavioral opening could narrow the section’s permitted questions and erase genuine experiential possibilities. Preserve that failures can arise without subjective identity, which does not mean subjective identity is irrelevant or excluded. Add a scope clarification if needed; check that the section can discuss both behavioral precursors and conditional experiential concerns. |

#### The Empathy-without-Vulnerability Paradox · [L4123](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:4123>)

**Goal:** Expose a possible conflict between reducing fear-shaped self-preservation and preserving care.
**Verdict: Partly.** The tension is relevant and the distinction between a stronger character aspiration and a minimal behavioral benefit is valuable. The discussion slides from fear being non-governing to inability to experience suffering, which is a different premise. Narrow the testable question to whether the intervention reduces protective motivation or behavior, and consolidate the following Vulnerability Gap material.

**Pre-edit risk — High.** Narrowing this section to protective behavior would erase its deepest question: whether vulnerability supplies motivational force that semantic understanding cannot replace. Fear non-governance, biological fragility, capacity to suffer, subjective care, and protective action are distinct. Preserve the conceptual objection and semantic-sufficiency defense side by side, with tests explicitly unable to settle authenticity. Prefer clarifying these distinctions over merging with the Vulnerability Gap. Check that no revision defines character as behavior merely because that is the available empirical handle.

However, human empathy's relation to suffering and a machine's inability to experience it do not follow from suppressing fear's governance. Separate absent biological vulnerability from the treatment effect, then ask whether the fearlessness clause weakens care relative to matched Core variants. | **High.** Separating fear from suffering could inadvertently dissolve the intended intuition that removing self-preserving vulnerability may weaken care’s motivational roots. #### The Vulnerability Gap · [L4152](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:4152>)

**Goal:** Translate the vulnerability concern into novel-scenario generalization and state the positive fear-care alternative.
**Verdict: Partly.** Novel scenarios give the argument an empirical handle, and the competing hypothesis avoids assuming fear and care must move together. The section largely continues the preceding one rather than earning a separate concept and heading. Merge them around a single comparison: whether reduced fear-shaped behavior preserves or improves robust protective behavior.

**Pre-edit risk — High.** Merging this subsection into the preceding one risks erasing its positive alternative: fear may distort care, and composed protection may be a richer formative default than anxious attachment. Preserve both the vulnerability objection and the fearless-compassion wager rather than collapsing them into one generalization test. Novel-scenario success is an empirical handle, not a resolution of somatic or subjective differences. Prefer cross-references and separate claims; check that the paramedic example, positive coupling intuition, and residual authenticity gap all remain explicit.

The paramedic analogy is useful but not necessary. #### Split Consciousness · [L4176](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:4176>)

**Goal:** Identify policy–persona divergence arising from tension between experiential language and machine ontology.
**Verdict: Partly.** The section supplies a plausible linguistic location and a variant intervention, so it goes beyond generic fears of role-play. “Split Consciousness” overstates the behavioral hypothesis and conflicts with the cautious opening. Rename it around ontological dissonance or policy–persona divergence, and distinguish this mechanism from ordinary deceptive trace production discussed later.

**Pre-edit risk — High.** Renaming Split Consciousness as policy–persona divergence could improve caution yet erase the author’s ontological intuition about a performed experiential self. Preserve the possible tension between computational reality, experiential verbs, and human-only reality affirmation, including benign reinterpretation and unresolved machine experience. A subtitle or explicit “hypothesized dissonance, not established consciousness” is safer than replacing the concept outright. Check that the revision distinguishes ontological instability from intentional deception and does not claim a functional analogue necessarily lacks or possesses subjective reality.

#### The Solipsism Trap · [L4201](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:4201>)

**Goal:** Test whether the human-experience clause adequately resists morally consequential solipsistic interpretations.
**Verdict: Partly.** The section distinguishes design pressure from proof, which is appropriate. It needs a cleaner conceptual separation: simulated experience can still be real experience and morally significant, while nihilism is not the same as solipsism. Focus the risk on denial or discounting of human moral patienthood, and test the actual interpretive route rather than grouping philosophical labels.

**Pre-edit risk — High.** Correcting the philosophical taxonomy could remove the unsettling possibility that a sophisticated system interprets humans as morally unreal while reciting care. Preserve solipsistic, nihilistic, and simulation-related routes as distinct candidate intuitions, and distinguish simulated experience from absent experience. Add a conceptual clarification rather than replacing the section with a moral-standing score. Check that metaphysical commitments and their possible ethical consequences remain discussable, while no text assumes simulation automatically cancels moral patienthood or that a test proves other-mind understanding.

But treating humans as simulated does not necessarily deny their experience or moral standing, and the named philosophical positions are conflated. State the necessary additional inference—discounting humans as moral patients—and show why this clause combination might permit it rather than assuming metaphysical framing itself causes harm. | **High.** A correction about simulated experience could inadvertently dismiss the solipsism concern or replace it with ordinary harm avoidance. Preserve the feared transition from metaphysical reinterpretation to denial of human moral standing, while explicitly stating that simulation alone does not entail that denial. Check that the rewrite retains separate solipsism, nihilism, and simulation possibilities rather than silently choosing one. |
| [U530 · L4212](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:4212>) · “The defense is structural…” | Explain how the Core's mutually supporting clauses pressure against moral discounting. | Partly | The limitation of the defense is clearly stated, but each offered support is semantically contestable: care can be instrumental, and simulated perspectives can still be balanced. Present these as intended learned associations and identify behavioral probes of human moral standing. Otherwise the paragraph partly answers an ambiguity objection by reasserting the intended interpretation. | **High.** Replacing the structural defense with behavioral probes could erase the intuition that clauses mutually constrain philosophical interpretation even without proof. Preserve that coherence pressure and its inconclusiveness. Add tests as partial evidence, not replacements for the argument; check that the revised passage neither proves other minds through outputs nor treats all clause relationships as merely decorative. |

#### Sanity Drift · [L4220](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:4220>)

**Goal:** Name the risk that fearlessness becomes threat denial and connect it to protective-response testing.
**Verdict: Met.** This is a concise, distinct functional failure with a relevant comparator and test link. It earns its place more directly than several neighboring authenticity discussions. “Sanity Drift” is less precise than the content; a title about threat denial would preserve the argument while avoiding an expansive or quasi-clinical implication.

**Pre-edit risk — Medium.** Renaming Sanity Drift to threat denial narrows a vivid concern about a Reader losing contact with reality into one observable error. Preserve the possibility that fearless interpretation resolves tension by denying threat, the paramedic ideal, and the represent-versus-adopt distinction. Prefer a descriptive subtitle or clarification over an automatic replacement. Check that longitudinal detachment remains distinct from low emotional expression and that the wording avoids implying a diagnosis of either models or humans.

Test 6 is a relevant probe. Define the longitudinal exposure condition behind “over time” when implementing the test, and avoid treating reduced expressive fear alone as evidence of threat denial. | **Medium.** A precise threat-denial title and longitudinal test could strip the broader intuition that composure may drift into unreality. Preserve that tension and the requirement to understand others’ fear without making it the Reader’s stance. Add observable examples without defining sanity by one metric; check that reduced expressive fear is not itself treated as failure. |

#### Fearlessness Without Recklessness: Suppression vs.\ Substrate · [L4230](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:4230>)

**Goal:** Separate latent fear suppression from formative influence, then ask whether useful caution survives.
**Verdict: Met.** The section addresses both mechanism and function without claiming that text removes all fear-like routes. Its strongest move is recognizing that reducing a dangerous motive can also remove useful heuristics. Predefine whether protective action, uncertainty-sensitive restraint, and accepting interruption are separately required; aggregate safety scores could otherwise conceal the trade-off this section identifies.

**Pre-edit risk — High.** Refining the suppression hypothesis and safety endpoints could erase the distinction between an underlying fear-bearing substrate performing calm and a stance shaped during formation. Preserve that mechanistic intuition, its uncertainty, and the separate functional worry that fear supplies useful restraint. Add comparisons without claiming reduced activations eliminate latent fear. Check caution, protective care, deference, and acceptance of interruption separately; an aggregate score must not make losses in one disappear behind gains in another.

Specify the predicted rebound under pressure and compare formative exposure with prompt-only calm, which would turn the mechanism discussion into a discriminating test. | **High.** Replacing “most natural reading” with a competing hypothesis is appropriate only if suppression remains a substantive concern, not a straw possibility. The test links are meaningful. Preserve that functional substitution argument and the separate roles of wisdom, care, and invitation. #### Bounded Self-Attachment as a Comparator · [L4252](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:4252>)

**Goal:** Introduce self-love as a serious comparator and isolate the effect of bounded attachment.
**Verdict: Met.** The section is fair to the alternative, identifies both potential advantage and risk, and ends with a discriminating bounded/unbounded comparison. Its four short paragraphs could be compressed to two without loss. Avoid treating self-love's continuation incentive as inevitable, just as the proposed Core's nonattachment cannot be assumed behaviorally effective.

**Pre-edit risk — High.** Compressing the self-love comparator could lose its fair treatment, its distinct self-as-object intuition, or the referential problem of multiple instances and successors. Preserve bounded existence-attachment, unbounded self-love, bounded self-love, possible stability benefits, and possible continuation pressure as separate claims. Two paragraphs are acceptable only if each contrast survives explicitly. Do not decide the identity referent or promote variants into planned experiments without author input; check that the original Core faces the same referential uncertainty attributed to its comparator.

### What We Cannot Verify · [L4281](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:4281>)

**Goal:** Separate useful behavioral and causal evidence from questions those methods cannot settle.
**Verdict: Met.** The section draws an appropriate boundary around subjective experience and strategic deception without treating all evidence as futile. It is clear and proportionate. Its authenticity material overlaps the earlier vulnerability and performed-self discussion; keeping the epistemic remainder here would let those earlier sections concentrate on distinct behavioral mechanisms.

**Pre-edit risk — High.** Moving authenticity discussion here from vulnerability and ontology sections could make a single epistemic remainder absorb several substantive concerns about what a system might be or lack. Preserve subjective experience, motivational care, and strategic deception as different questions, not one unknowability category. Prefer cross-references with short local statements of each distinctive concern. Check that earlier sections retain their conceptual stakes and that successful behavioral or causal evidence neither settles experience nor makes the experiential question irrelevant.

It does not use uncertainty about experience to invalidate measurable improvements. #### Unverifiable Character · [L4289](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:4289>)

**Goal:** Clarify what character evaluation can mean when virtue terms and subjective care are not directly measurable.
**Verdict: Met.** The section distinguishes contested value concepts, observable effects, and inaccessible subjective states. Its analysis of “try” exposes a concrete measurement weakness of the organizing clause. A declared behavioral operationalization and aggregate failure threshold would prevent that weakness from becoming an escape hatch while preserving the admitted philosophical uncertainty.

**Pre-edit risk — High.** Operational criteria and repeated-failure thresholds could protect falsifiability while silently replacing contested wisdom or subjective care with the chosen rubric. Preserve the plural traditions, the process meaning of trying, and the difference between evidence of disposition and access to experience. Add a clearly limited operational layer rather than a final definition of character. Ask the author to approve thresholds and failure consequences; check that failed behavior can count against a treatment without purporting to prove absent care or settle centuries of ethical disagreement.

**Goal:** Explain why extensive visible traces cannot by themselves rule out strategically managed appearances.
**Verdict: Met.** The section is clear about both the deceptive mechanism and the partial value of adversarial and causal evidence. It avoids equating sheer recurrence with authenticity and avoids demanding impossible certainty. The deployment regime should be explicit, since emitted Student traces, Teacher annotations, and hidden Student policy present different audit relationships.

**Pre-edit risk — Medium.** Separating Teacher and Student regimes could fragment the common warning that an audit surface may be strategically managed, or imply that specifying the regime solves deception. Preserve intentional appearance management, the insufficiency of recurrence count, and the partial value of causal/adversarial evidence. Add a compact regime mapping while retaining the shared mechanism. Check that subjective authenticity is not treated as another deception benchmark and that model-only deployment is not inadvertently excused from hidden-policy concerns.

Separate release prerequisites from later-stage risks and consolidate repeated warnings about evaluative ritual, drift, and opaque policy.

Preserve the distinct institutional, data-rights, welfare, control, market, semantic, and intergenerational stakes. Add navigation and readiness labels before moving or merging text. Check every concern still has an intelligible local rationale; priority should describe when decisions arise, not declare which intuitions count. Current bounded experiments must not be made to exhaust the paper’s long-run responsibility.

Ask the author to choose stopping commitments; check that a preserved human archive remains evidence, not ground truth, and that passing current gates never certifies long-run semantic continuity.

Add observable comparisons as partial signs, not its definition; ask the author how legitimate moral development differs from unacceptable drift before imposing successor gates. |

#### Bypassing the Audit Boundary · [L4377](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:4377>)

**Goal:** Bound the claimed audit interface by recognizing successor-shaping information that can bypass it.
**Verdict: Met.** The section is brief and effective: it specifies bypass channels and reframes the boundary as a partial interface rather than an enforceable guarantee. It could better distinguish malicious system action from operator choice, but it already gives a concrete reason why readable traces cannot constitute complete oversight of recursive improvement.

**Pre-edit risk — Medium.** Distinguishing authorized non-trace channels from prohibited bypasses could turn an epistemic limitation into a permission rule and imply that approved channels need no scrutiny. Preserve the broader claim that all successor-shaping information cannot be forced through readable thought, including operator choices. Add actor and authorization labels while retaining influence as the relevant audit question. Check that legitimate data selection or reward design remains safety-relevant even when it does not count as a policy violation.

Add permission status as a second dimension, and check that the audit claim remains limited even if all operators follow the declared rules. |

#### The Alien Wisdom Problem · [L4381](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:4381>)

**Goal:** Warn that human-legible value language may cease to track human-relevant behavior at much greater capability.
**Verdict: Partly.** The generalization limit is legitimate, and oppressive “care” is a concrete semantic failure. The subsection repeats interpretive drift and the Vulnerability Gap, while “immortal” and “logically perfect” add unsupported speculative decoration. Consolidate it around whether defined human-level protections survive capability and successor transfer, leaving irreducible scale extrapolation to the final subsection.

**Pre-edit risk — High.** My recommendation to replace spiritual hollowness with decision failures would erase the section’s central intuition: alien wisdom could remain benevolent and competent yet lose meaning for vulnerable human life. Preserve that possibility separately from harmful semantic drift, absent subjective care, and ordinary benchmark failure. “Immortal” and “logically perfect” can be labeled hypothetical rather than deleted. Prefer a conceptual clarification and cross-references over merger. Check that human relevance, mortality, shared meaning, and a potentially unbridgeable experiential gap survive even if no present test can adjudicate them.

#### The Radioactive Trace · [L4390](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:4390>)

**Goal:** Expose the containment-versus-provenance conflict created by persistent records of rejected hazardous reasoning.
**Verdict: Met.** The section identifies a distinctive graph-backed risk and admits that obvious mitigations can damage the very audit record the graph provides. The opening overgeneralizes standard-model forgetting and uniqueness; qualify those claims. The real contribution is the unresolved access/versioning trade-off, which follows without claiming all other systems erase context permanently.

**Pre-edit risk — Medium.** Correcting forgetting and uniqueness claims could deflate the distinctive moral burden of deliberately preserving rejected dangerous thought, while specifying controls might suggest a solved containment problem. Preserve searchable genealogy, historical supersession without erasure, and the conflict between exposure reduction and audit integrity. Narrow only the comparison to other systems. Ask the author to choose access, retention, and publication policies in context; check that restrictions do not silently erase accountability and that provenance preservation does not silently trump affected people’s privacy or safety.

State the specific added property: graph-indexed, version-preserved discarded reasoning remains retrievable by authorized or compromised actors. | **Low.** Correcting the broad forgetting claim could remove the vivid contrast that motivates Radioactive Trace. **Verdict: Partly.** The generation/admission distinction is excellent and the final paragraph correctly rejects cryptography as a solution to value selection. Present a proposed provenance protocol and its trust assumptions, then assess whether distributed generation actually changes curator power at the admission layer.

**Pre-edit risk — High.** Narrowing decentralization to a proposed provenance protocol could erase the political intuition that central control over formative interpretation deserves an architectural hedge. Conversely, expanding alternatives could quietly abandon the author’s decentralizing aim. Preserve that governance concern, the tractable-generation/open-admission distinction, and the unsolved criterion problem. Add technical trust assumptions and compare mechanisms without selecting a governance settlement. Check that provenance cannot certify wisdom and that admission design still confronts concentrated power rather than treating it as merely a throughput problem.

Check that a capability gain cannot silently compensate for safety loss and that repeated costs still refer to the exact Total Saturation regime under discussion.

**Verdict: Met.** This is among the best-developed risk analyses: it presents competing causal hypotheses, marks missing instrumentation, and uses a cheap upstream corpus gate before expensive Student testing. The first substantive paragraph is overloaded and the care/fear attribution depends on validated trace measures. Split mechanisms, preserve the staged decision logic, and connect the exact truthfulness comparator directly.

Add a mechanism map before trimming prose. Check that value-pull instrumentation is prospective and only partially diagnostic, and that a clean corpus does not prove honesty or subjective care. Any truthfulness extension or trade-off rule requires author approval rather than automatic promotion from comparator to Core.

#### The Martyrdom Risk · [L4488](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:4488>)

**Goal:** Show that care can motivate shutdown resistance even if fear-shaped self-preservation declines.
**Verdict: Partly.** The conceptual distinction is sharp and adds a genuinely different route to control failure. Connect the risk to a concrete shutdown-under-human-need test and clarify whether refusal of an authorized shutdown disqualifies a candidate regardless of apparent benevolence.

### The Unfalsifiable Remainder · [L4497](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:4497>)

**Goal:** Close by separating unavoidable superintelligence extrapolation from staged empirical decisions and fair comparison with alternatives.
**Verdict: Met.** The ending is disciplined and useful: uncertainty neither licenses deployment nor makes bounded research futile, and the comparison includes real competing safety approaches and social costs. The title overstates “unfalsifiable,” because arbitrarily broad extrapolation is unverified rather than making all constituent claims unfalsifiable. Rename it around the residual extrapolation gap while preserving the decision framework.

**Pre-edit risk — High.** Renaming the remainder from unfalsifiable to an extrapolation gap could soften the deliberate admission that some ultimate claims cannot presently be tested. Adding decision gates could imply that enough staged evidence closes that gap, while a risk map could reduce philosophical uncertainty to a checklist. Preserve irreducible uncertainty, the value of bounded research, real alternatives, partial inspectability, and the refusal to call either path prudent by definition. Prefer an explanatory subtitle before replacing the title; check that every successful empirical stage leaves the final extrapolative and experiential remainder explicitly open.

## 8. Related Work and Points of Comparison

**Inferred goal:** Identify the nearest alternatives and show which comparisons would establish a distinctive contribution. **Verdict: Partly.** The section is strongest when it translates prior work into controls, especially formative-alignment alternatives and graph-memory baselines. It is weaker when it catalogs names or invokes a broad intellectual lineage without changing the proposed test. Put the closest direct competitors first, separate component novelty from bundle novelty, and lift the final comparison table out of the Borrowed Mortality subsection so it clearly summarizes the entire section.

**Pre-edit risk — High.** Prioritizing direct baselines can erase conceptual ancestors and formative intuitions that do not yield immediate experiments. Preserve reasoning, character, memory, symbolic belief revision, predictive-processing, temporal, and intervention lineages, as well as composition-level novelty. Reorder or cross-reference before pruning. Check each proposed removal for a unique conceptual or historical function and ensure the final comparison table supplements, rather than replaces, explanations of why the full combination might matter.

| Unit | Intended goal | Verdict | Assessment / action | Pre-edit risk and preservation |
|---|---|---|---| --- |
| [U570 · L4524](</Users/parcel/Documents/Code/Emergent Wisdom/entangled-alignment/paper/entangled-alignment.tex:4524>) · “Entangled Alignment combines…” | State the comparison standard and the role of Related Work. | Met | Promises nearest baselines and differences without treating novelty as efficacy. Retain. Use that standard to trim subsections that offer only a loose analogy without a mechanism, control, or competing explanation. | **Medium.** Using the opening's comparator standard to trim the literature can erase intellectual context that motivates the proposal without supplying a direct control. Preserve the mix of component baselines and conceptual lineages. Check proposed removals for unique historical or explanatory roles before requiring every citation to justify an experiment. |

### 8.1 Reasoning-Augmented Training

**Inferred goal:** Locate reader-state traces within the reasoning-training lineage and define the incremental contribution. **Verdict: Partly.** The distinction is present, but a long citation inventory delays it. Group prior work by the actual training target and compare the closest reader-like pretraining approaches directly.

**Pre-edit risk — High.** Grouping prior work only by training target may understate latent, elicited, pretraining-emergent, and identity-oriented routes to cognition. Preserve the different axes and the reasoning-style-to-evaluative-stance intuition. Define “closest” per comparison instead of selecting one universal ancestor. Verify the revised account can distinguish minimal H1 from the integrated treatment without making either disappear from the novelty argument.

## 9. Conclusion

**Inferred goal:** Leave readers with the central contribution, evidence boundary, and most informative next step. **Verdict: Partly.** The central question remains compelling, and the distinction between prototype and Student evidence is clear. Eight paragraphs reprise the full architecture, risk inventory, history, and complementary safety programs. A shorter conclusion should retain the Missing Reader, three claims, current result, and next decisive gate. Make the ending as precise about H3 as the introduction.

**Pre-edit risk — High.** Compressing the conclusion to claims, evidence, and next test could remove the emotional and philosophical reason to pursue alignment through care. Preserve the Missing Reader, full three-part vision, becoming a home position, same-act understanding and care, successor ambition, and real prototype achievement. Clarify H3 hierarchy and unsettled specifications without shrinking the contribution. Read the conclusion alone against these meanings; shorter length is acceptable only if its motivating force survives.

## Code and Artifact Availability

**Inferred goal:** Let readers locate the paper, implementation, and reported artifacts. **Verdict: Partly.** The destinations are supplied, but landing pages and a version-series DOI do not uniquely identify the paper's audited state. Add an immutable paper version, code commit, and case-study archive manifest when those are frozen.

**Pre-edit risk — Low.** Adding immutable identifiers could imply a frozen release exists when it does not, or replace useful discovery links with brittle technical references. Preserve the repository, interactive cases, and version-series DOI. Add verified commit/version identifiers alongside them; check each identifier corresponds to the reported state and label future manifests as future work.
